P1 MPL architecture; ZCP stack O-FG-013 Resolved: (1) Methods: Weighted Average and FIFO only. (2) Item overrides group; default Weighted Average. (5) Period lock by date; unlock needs right + reason + audit. (6) Orders never reserve stock (D-M6 = No). (7) Golden ledgers: opening, purchase, sale, returns, adjustment, negative-stock sale, lock/unlock (Decided: proposed value) Resolved: Money INTEGER paise.
